The command is part of `cli/commands/account.py` and shares its verb table with add, get and rename.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. `ledger account delete <code>` takes exactly one argument; any other count is a usage error (exit 2).\n2. An absent code exits 4 with nothing on standard output.\n3. An account referenced by any transaction is refused with exit 3 and nothing changes.\n4. Otherwise the account row is deleted and the command prints `deleted` then the code, one per line.\n5. The in-use check and the delete happen before any other write and in one database transaction.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC acct-delete-unused ===\nIntent: Deleting an account with no transactions prints deleted and the code, and a separate read finds it gone.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZADEL1\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=code, name=\"Delete Me\")\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", code)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert exit_code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows == [[\"deleted\"], [code]]\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=code) is None\n=== END AC acct-delete-unused ===\n\n=== AC acct-delete-in-use ===\nIntent: An account referenced by a transaction is refused with exit 3, writes nothing db.accounts.create(account=code, name=\"In Use\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"In Use Cat\")\n    existing = db.transactions.list()\n    txn_id = max([r.txn_id for r in existing], default=0) + 1\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-05-01\", account=code, category=cat, amount=500, memo=\"held\"\n    )\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"delete\", code)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert exit_code == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=code) is not None\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=txn_id) is not None\n=== END AC acct-delete-in-use ===\n\n=== AC acct-delete-absent ===\nIntent: Deleting an account that does not exist exits 4 with nothing on standard ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TxnPost.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Post\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger txn post` with validation, id allocation and the non-negative balance rule. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LedgerRules.md, FEATURE-LoadCategories.md, FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-Validation.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes | ledgerlib.rules, ledgerlib.validation, ledgerlib.csvout |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRecord one receipt or payment against an account. The command lives in `cli/commands/txn.py`, which owns the verb table for `txn post|get|amend|delete`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Usage: `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]`. Fewer than four or more than five arguments is a usage error (exit 2). The memo defaults to the empty string.\n2. Every input is validated before any write: codes, amount (integer cents, never zero, at most two decimals), real calendar date, and memo rules. Violations exit 3.\n3. The account and the category must exist, else exit 3.\n4. The projected balance of the account after the post must not be negative, else exit 3.\n5. The new `txn_id` is the largest present plus one, or 1 when empty. The id is read and the row written in one database transaction.\n6. Output is the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the new row, amount with exactly two decimals.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-success ===\nIntent: A valid post returns the next id and the row, and a separate read returns the same stored values in integer cents.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZZTPOST1\"\ncat = \"ZZTPCAT1\"\namount_arg = \"45.50\"\ndate = \"2026-04-01\"\nmemo = \"walk in sale\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Post One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Post Cat\")\n    before = max([r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()], default=0)\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, amount_arg, date, memo)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert exit_code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"txn_id\", \"date\", \"account\", \"category\", \"amount\", \"memo\"]\n    new_id = before + 1\n    assert rows[1] == [str(new_id), date, acct, cat, amount_arg, memo]\n    back = db.transactions.get(txn_id=new_id)\n    assert back is not None\n    assert back.account == acct\n    assert back.category == cat\n    assert back.date == date\n    assert back.memo == memo\n    assert back.amount == 4550\n=== END AC txn-post-success ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-default-memo-and-ids ===\nIntent: A post without a memo stores an empty memo, and two consecutive posts receive consecutive ids.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZZTPOST2\"\ncat = \"ZZTPCAT2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Post Two\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Post Cat Two\")\n    before = max([r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()], default=0)\n    c1, o1, e1 = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, \"10.00\", \"2026-04-02\")\n    c2, o2, e2 = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, \"-3.00\", \"2026-04-03\", \"second\")\n    print(o1, e1, o2, e2)\n    assert c1 == 0\n    assert c2 == 0\n    first = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(o1)))[1]\n    second = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(o2)))[1]\n    assert first[0] == str(before + 1)\n    assert second[0] == str(before + 2)\n    assert first[5] == \"\"\n    stored = db.transactions.get(txn_id=before + 1)\n    assert stored is not None\n    assert stored.memo == \"\"\n    other = db.transactions.get(txn_id=before + 2)\n    assert other is not None\n    assert other.amount == -300\n=== END AC txn-post-ids ===\n\n=== AC ]\n    for args in cases:\n        exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", *args)\n        print(args, exit_code, out, err)\n        assert exit_code == 3\n        assert out == \"\"\n ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-TxnPost.md ===\n# FEATURE: Transaction Post\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger txn post` with validation, id allocation and the non-negative balance rule. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LedgerRules.md, FEATURE-LoadCategories.md, FEATURE-AccountAdd.md, FEATURE-Validation.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger txn post |\n| Consumes | ledgerlib.rules, ledgerlib.validation, ledgerlib.csvout |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRecord one receipt or payment against an account. The command lives in `cli/commands/txn.py`, which owns the verb table for `txn post|get|amend|delete`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Usage: `ledger txn post <account> <category> <amount> <date> [<memo>]`. Fewer than four or more than five arguments is a usage error (exit 2). The memo defaults to the empty string.\n2. Every input is validated before any write: codes, amount (integer cents, never zero, at most two decimals), real calendar date, and memo rules. Violations exit 3.\n3. The account and the category must exist, else exit 3.\n4. The projected balance of the account after the post must not be negative, else exit 3.\n5. The new `txn_id` is the largest present plus one, or 1 when empty. The id is read and the row written in one database transaction.\n6. Output is the header `txn_id,date,account,category,amount,memo` and the new row, amount with exactly two decimals.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC txn-post-success ===\nIntent: A valid post returns the next id and the row, and a separate read returns the same stored values in integer cents.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZZTPOST1\"\ncat = \"ZZTPCAT1\"\namount_arg = \"45.50\"\ndate = \"2026-04-01\"\nmemo = \"walk in sale\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Post One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Post Cat\")\n    before = max([r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()], default=0)\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"post\", acct, cat, amount_arg, date, memo)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert exit_code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    assert rows[0] == [\"txn_id\", \"date\", \"account\", \"category\", \"amount\", \"memo\"]\n    new_id = before + 1\n    assert rows[1] == [str(new_id), date, acct, cat, amount_arg, memo]\n    back = db.transactions.get(txn_id=new_id)\n    assert back is not None\n    assert back.account == acct\n    assert back.category == cat\n    assert back.date == date\n    assert back.memo == memo\n    assert back.amount == 4550\n=== END AC txn-post-success ===\n\n=== AC txn-post-default-memo-and-ids ===\nIntent: A post without a memo stores an empty memo, and two consecutive posts receive consecutive ids.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZZTPOST2\"\ncat = \"ZZTPCAT2\"\nwith stored.memo == \"\"\n    other = db.transactions.get(txn_id=before + 2)\n    assert other is not None\n    assert other.amount == -300\n=== END AC txn-post-ids ===\n\n=== AC unchanged.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nsrc = \"ZZTDEL3A\"\ndst = \"ZZTDEL3B\"\ncat = \"ZZTDCAT3\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=src, name=\"Leg Source\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=dst, name=\"Leg Dest\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Leg Cat\")\n    base = max([r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()], default=0) + 1\n    seed_id, out_id, in_id = base, base + 1, base + 2\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=seed_id, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=src, category=cat, amount=1000, memo=\"seed\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=out_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=src, category=cat, amount=-300, memo=\"leg out\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=in_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=dst, category=cat, amount=300, memo=\"leg in\"\n    )\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", str(in_id))\n    print(out, err)\n    assert exit_code == 0\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=in_id) is None\n    paired = db.transactions.get(txn_id=out_id)\n    assert paired is not None\n    assert paired.amount == -300\n    assert paired.account == src\n=== END AC txn-delete-transfer-leg ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-absent ===\nIntent: Deleting an id no transaction holds exits 4 with nothing on standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    missing = max([r.txn_id for r in db.transactions.list()], default=0) + 1000\n    exit_code, out, err = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", str(missing))\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert exit_code == 4\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-delete-absent ===\n\n=== AC txn-delete-bad-input ===\nIntent: A non-digit id exits 3 and a missing id is a usage error with exit 2.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode_bad, out_bad, err_bad = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\", \"abc\")\nprint(out_bad, err_bad)\nassert code_bad == 3\nassert out_bad == \"\"\ncode_usage, out_usage, err_usage = cli_runner(\"txn\", \"delete\")\nprint(out_usage, err_usage)\nassert code_usage == 2\nassert out_usage == \"\"\n=== END AC txn-delete-bad-input ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The balance check
